    function listminicart(cellid,tomorrow){
		
        var urlpath = 'https://www.promessedefleurs.com/dailydeal/deal/ajaxdeal/?currenttime='+tomorrow;	
        new Ajax.Request(urlpath,{
            method: "GET",
            onSuccess:	function(resp){
                $('cell-'+cellid).addTip(resp.responseText, {style: 'grouped'});
            }
        });
    }
    
    function countDownSecond(obj) {
            
        if( obj.second > 0){
            obj.second--;
            
            setTimeout(function() {
                if(obj.callback != ''){
                    obj.callback(obj);
                }else{
                    showTime(obj);
                }
            }, 0);
            
            setTimeout(function() {
                countDownSecond(obj);
            }, 1000);
        }
    }
    
    function showTime(obj) {
    
        var seconds = Math.floor(obj.second);
        var minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
        var hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
        var days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
        
        hours %= 24;
        minutes %= 60;
        seconds %= 60;
        
        var str_days = wrapperTagSpan ( insertOneZero(days) );
        var str_hours = wrapperTagSpan ( insertOneZero(hours) );
        var str_minutes = wrapperTagSpan ( insertOneZero(minutes) );
        var str_seconds = wrapperTagSpan ( insertOneZero(seconds) );
        
        if ($(obj.label_day)){
            if(obj.label_day_value){
                
                $(obj.label_day).innerHTML     = obj.label_day_value;
            }else{
                $(obj.label_day).innerHTML   = 'jours';
            }
        }
            
        if ($(obj.label_hour)) {
            if(obj.label_hour_value){
                $(obj.label_hour).innerHTML     = obj.label_hour_value;
            }else{
                $(obj.label_hour).innerHTML     = 'Hours';
            }
        }
            
        if ($(obj.label_minute)){
            if(obj.label_minute_value){
                $(obj.label_minute).innerHTML     = obj.label_minute_value;
            }else{
                $(obj.label_minute).innerHTML   = 'Minutes';
            }
        }
            
        if ($(obj.label_second)){
            if(obj.label_second_value){
                $(obj.label_second).innerHTML     = obj.label_second_value;
            }else{
                $(obj.label_second).innerHTML   = 'Seconds';
            }
        }
            
        if($(obj.id_day)) $(obj.id_day).innerHTML    = str_days;
        if($(obj.id_hour)) $(obj.id_hour).innerHTML    = str_hours;
        if($(obj.id_minute)) $(obj.id_minute).innerHTML    = str_minutes;
        if($(obj.id_second)) $(obj.id_second).innerHTML    = str_seconds;
            
        if(days <= 0){
            if ($(obj.label_day)) $(obj.label_day).innerHTML     = '';
            if($(obj.id_day)) $(obj.id_day).innerHTML    = '';
        }
        if(days <= 0 && hours <=0){
            if ($(obj.label_hour)) $(obj.label_hour).innerHTML     = '';
            if($(obj.id_hour)) $(obj.id_hour).innerHTML    = '';
        }
    }
    
    function insertOneZero(value) {
        var result = '';
            
        if(value < 10){
            result += '0' + value;
        }else{
            result += value;
        }
        
        return result;
    }
    
    function wrapperTagSpan(string) {
        var result = '';
        
        string.toString();
        
        for(var i=0; i<string.length; i++) {
            result += "<span>" + string.charAt(i)+"</span>";
        }

        return result;
    }

</script>

<form action="https://www.promessedefleurs.com/solrsearch/result/" id="search_mini_form" method="get">
<div class="form-search">
<label for="search">Rechercher :</label>
<input autocomplete="off" class="input-text" id="search" maxlength="128" name="q" placeholder="Rechercher" type="text" value=""/>
<button class="button" id="search-submit-btn" title="Chercher" type="submit">
<i class="icon-search"></i>
</button>
<span id="search-loading" style="display:none;"></span>
<div class="solrsearch-autocomplete" id="solrsearch_autocomplete"></div>
<script type="defer">
            //<![CDATA[
            var wcUpdateSuggestResultTimeout = false;
            var searched = false;
            var typing = false;
            //var searchId = 0;
            var cacheResults = {};
            var pendingRequest;
            
            function closeAutocompletionBox() {
                jQuery('#solrsearch_autocomplete').slideUp(100);
                if (jQuery('body').hasClass('touch-device'))
                    jQuery('body,html').css('overflow', 'visible');
            }
            
            function openAutocompletionBox() {
                jQuery('#solrsearch_autocomplete').slideDown(100);
                if (jQuery('body').hasClass('touch-device'))
                    jQuery('body,html').css('overflow', 'hidden');
            }
            
            function wcAutocomplete(event) {
                $('search-loading').hide();
                $('search-submit-btn').show();
                switch (event.keyCode) {
                    case Event.KEY_TAB:
                    case Event.KEY_LEFT:
                    case Event.KEY_RIGHT:
                    case Event.KEY_ENTER:
                        return;
                    case Event.KEY_UP:
                        //todo select prev
                        return;
                    case Event.KEY_DOWN:
                        //todo select next
                        return;
                    case Event.KEY_ESC:
                        //close todo
                        return;
                        
                    case 16://shift
                    case 17://ctrl
                    case 18://alt
                    case 20://maj
                    case 91://cmd
                        //do nothing
                        return;
                }
                clearTimeout(wcUpdateSuggestResultTimeout);
                value = $('search').getValue();
                if(!value) {
                    resetSolrSearchBox();
                    return;
                }
                if (value.length >= 3) {
                    wcUpdateSuggestResultTimeout = setTimeout(wcUpdateSuggestResult, 400);
                }
            }
            
            function wcUpdateSuggestResult() {
                if(pendingRequest) {
                    pendingRequest.transport.abort();
                }
                $('search-submit-btn').hide();
                $('search-loading').show();
                value = $('search').getValue();
                if(cacheResults[value]) {
                    $('solrsearch_autocomplete').update(cacheResults[value]);
                    $('search-loading').hide();
                    $('search-submit-btn').show();
                    
                } else {
                    //searchId++;
                    pendingRequest = new Ajax.Request('https://www.promessedefleurs.com/solrsearch/ajax/suggest/', {
                        method: 'get',   
                        parameters : {q: value, edito:location.href.match(/conseil-plantes-jardin/i) ? 1: 0/*, searchId:searchId*/},
                        evalScripts : true,
                        onComplete: function(response) {
                            //if(response.request.parameters.searchId == searchId) {
                                cacheResults[value] = response.responseText;
                                $('solrsearch_autocomplete').update(response.responseText);                               
                                $('search-loading').hide();
                                $('search-submit-btn').show();
                            //}
                        }
                    });
                }
                openAutocompletionBox();
                searched = true;
                $('solrsearch_autocomplete').addClassName('active');
            }
            
            function resetSolrSearchBox() {
                 $('solrsearch_autocomplete').removeClassName('active');
                 $('solrsearch_autocomplete').innerHTML = '';
                 searched = false;
            }
            
            Event.observe($('search'), 'keyup', wcAutocomplete);
            
            document.observe('click', function(e, el) {
                if (!e.target.descendantOf('search_mini_form'))
                    closeAutocompletionBox();
            });
            
            document.observe('click', function(e, el) {
                if (e.target.descendantOf('search_mini_form') && searched)
                    jQuery('#solrsearch_autocomplete').is(':visible') ? closeAutocompletionBox() : openAutocompletionBox();
            });

            //var searchForm = new Varien.searchForm('search_mini_form', 'search', 'Rechercher...');
            //searchForm.initAutocomplete('https://www.promessedefleurs.com/solrsearch/ajax/suggest/', 'search_autocomplete');
            //]]>
        </script>

<h2><span>Le spécialiste des plantes de jardin</span></h2>
</div>
<div class="choisirpdf">
<p>Promesse de fleurs vous propose, depuis 1950, une très large gamme de plantes de qualité pour le jardin, <a href="vivaces.html" title="&lt;?php echo $this-&gt;__('Vivaces') ?&gt;">vivaces</a>, <a href="arbustes.html" title="&lt;?php echo $this-&gt;__('Arbustes') ?&gt;">arbustes</a>, <a href="grimpantes.html" title="&lt;?php echo $this-&gt;__('Grimpantes') ?&gt;">grimpantes</a>, <a href="rosiers.html" title="&lt;?php echo $this-&gt;__('Rosiers') ?&gt;">rosiers</a>, <a href="bulbes-de-printemps.html" title="&lt;?php echo $this-&gt;__('Bulbes de printemps') ?&gt;">bulbes à fleurs</a>, <a href="annuelles.html" title="&lt;?php echo $this-&gt;__('Annuelles') ?&gt;">plantes annuelles </a>, et <a href="graines.html" title="&lt;?php echo $this-&gt;__('Graines') ?&gt;">graines</a>, sélectionnées pour leur beauté et leur robustesse. Nous vous conseillons dans le choix, la culture et le soin de vos plantes, et vous proposons des mises en situation dans des sélections de style, de couleur et de terroir.</p>
<p>Les plantes commandées avant midi sont expédiées le jour même ou au moment de votre choix, exclusivement depuis notre pépinière, à Houplines, dans le Nord de la France, fraîches, soigneusement contrôlées et bien emballées en coques de protection. Vous pouvez également commander nos plantes en ligne et venir les chercher dans le Jardin Drive accolé à notre pépinière; vous économiserez ainsi les frais de port, 6.90€, et pourrez en profiter pour visiter nos serres, qui abritent le plus grand choix de bulbes à fleurs et de plantes de jardin en France.</p>
<p>Avec un grand souci de service et de respect de nos clients jardiniers, nous garantissons la reprise et le bon développement de chacune de nos plantes.</p>
<span class="more" onclick="javascript: jQuery('.choisirpdf').addClass('showmore');">Voir plus</span></div> </div>
</div>

//<![CDATA[
   var Man4xLazyloadClass = (function ($) {
        var instance;
        
        var defaultLazySelector = ".lazy";
        
        var defaultLazyloadConfig = {
            defaultImage: 'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mPkOSxdDwADOAFrhx3IyQAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==',
            bind: 'event',
            effect: 'fadeIn',
            effectTime: 400,
            chainable: false,
            afterLoad: function(element) {
                $(element).css('display', 'inline-block') // To cancel display switching to 'block' caused by fadeIn effect
                        .parents('.lazyload-wrap')
                        .removeClass('loading')
                        .css("padding-bottom", 0);
            },
            onError: function(element) {
                console.log('Error lazy loading ' + element.context.dataset.src);
                $(element).css('display', 'none')
                        .parents('.lazyload-wrap')
                        .removeClass('loading')
                        .css("padding-bottom", 0);
            },
            beforeLoad: function(element) {}
        };

        function init() {
            function lazyload(selector, config) {
                config = config || {};
                var lazyloadConfig = $.extend({}, defaultLazyloadConfig, config);
                var lazyload = $(selector || defaultLazySelector).Lazy(lazyloadConfig);
                return lazyload;
            }
            
            var man4xLazyload;            

            // Force images included in ajax reloaded content (triggered by jQuery) to be lazyloaded at parsing
            $(document).ajaxSuccess(
                function() {
                    Man4xLazyload.start();
                }
            );
    
           return {                
                start: function() {
                    if (man4xLazyload)
                        man4xLazyload.destroy();
                    man4xLazyload = lazyload();
                },
                
                add: function(selector, force) {
                    force = force && true;
                    man4xLazyload.addItems(selector);
                    if (force)
                        man4xLazyload.force(selector);
                },
                
                break: function() {
                    man4xLazyload.loadAll();
                },
                
                new: function(selector, config) {
                    return lazyload(selector, config);
                }
            };
        };

        return {
            getInstance: function () {
                if (!instance)
                    instance = init();

                return instance;
            }

        };
    })(jQuery);

    Man4xLazyload = Man4xLazyloadClass.getInstance();
    Man4xLazyload.start();
    
    // Force images included in ajax reloaded content (triggered by Prototype) to be lazyloaded at parsing
    Ajax.Responders.register({
        onComplete: function() {
            Man4xLazyload.start();
        }
    });
//]]>

//<![CDATA[
   var deferAllSingleton = (function () {
        var instance;
        
        function init() {
            function download(files, callback) {
                var script, attr;
                while (script = files.shift()) {
                    var element = document.createElement("script");
                    element.async = false;
                    element.src = script['url'];
                    if (script['params']) {
                        var attrs = script['params'].match(/\S+/g) || [];
                        while (attr = attrs.shift()) {
                            var attrVal = attr.split('=');
                            if (attrVal.length === 2) {
                                element[trim(attrVal[0])] = attrVal[1].replace(/['"]+/g, '');
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (!files.length && callback) {
                        element.onload = element.onerror = function() {
                            try {deferAllSingleton.getInstance()[callback]();}
                                catch (e) {alert(e);}
                        };
                    }
                    else {
                        element.onload = function() {console.log(this.src + ' loaded !');}
                    }
                    document.body.appendChild(element);
		}
            }
            
            function restoreInlineJs() {
                console.time("Restoring Inline JS");
                var inlineJs = '';
                $$('script[type="defer"]').each(
                    function(script) {
                        inlineJs += script.innerHTML;        
                    }
                );
                $(document.body).insert(new Element('script').update(inlineJs));
                console.timeEnd("Restoring Inline JS");
            }
            
            function restoreCss() {
                console.time("Restoring CSS");               
                $$('script[media=defer]').invoke('writeAttribute', 'media', 'all');
                console.timeEnd("Restoring CSS");
            }
